<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

Well that's a first for me: an NTP Pool server sent a wildly wrong time, and systemd-timesyncd jumped the system time on Trastevere first forward 0.82s and then *backwards* 0.76s. Thankfully Sunlight failed safe, crashed (which fired alerts), and recovered once the clock caught up. WTF?
